void mpiCheck(int error) {
    if (error == MPI_SUCCESS) return;
    char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
    int length = 0;
    MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
    fprintf(stderr, \"MPI: %.*s\\n\", length, message);
    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, error);
}

void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error) {
    if (error "'!= cudaSuccess) throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(error));
}

struct ParallelRuntime {
    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
    MPI_Comm localComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    ParallelRuntime(int& argc, char**& argv) {
        int provided = 0;
        mpiCheck(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
            fprintf(stderr, "MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED is required.'"\\n\");
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        }
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
                                    MPI_INFO_NULL, &localComm));
    }
    void selectDevice() const {
        int localRank = 0, devices = 0;
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_rank(localComm, &localRank));
        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
        if ("'!devices) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA accelerator is required on every rank");
        // Also supports schedulers that expose just one assigned GPU per process.
        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
    }
    ~ParallelRuntime() {
        MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
        MPI_Finalize();
    }
};

template<class T> class DeviceArray {
    T* data_ = nullptr;
public:
    DeviceArray() = default;
    DeviceArray(const DeviceArray&) = delete;
    DeviceArray& operator=(const DeviceArray&) = delete;
    void resize(size_t n) {
        if (data_) cudaCheck(cudaFree(data_));
        data_ = nullptr;
        if (n > SIZE_MAX / sizeof(T)) throw std::runtime_error("Device allocation size overflow");
        if (n) cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), n * sizeof(T)));
    }
    T* get() const { return data_; }
    ~DeviceArray() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
};

template<class T> class PinnedArray {
    T* data_ = nullptr;
public:
    void resize(size_t n) {
        if (data_) cudaCheck(cudaFreeHost(data_));
        data_ = nullptr;
        if (n > SIZE_MAX / sizeof(T)) throw std::runtime_error("Host allocation size overflow");
        // Allocate one element even on ranks owning no receiver rows.
        cudaCheck(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), std::max(n, size_t(1)) * sizeof(T)));
    }
    T* get() const { return data_; }
    ~PinnedArray() { if (data_) cudaFreeHost(data_); }
};

struct FlatNode {
    Vec3 center, halfExtent;
    uint32_t begin, count, escape;
};

void flattenOctree(const Octree& tree, std::vector<FlatNode>& nodes,
                   std::vector<idx_t>& indices) {
    const size_t here = nodes.size();
    nodes.push_back({tree.center, tree.halfExtent, static_cast<uint32_t>(indices.size()),
                     static_cast<uint32_t>(tree.triangleIndices.size()), 0});
    for (size_t i : tree.triangleIndices) indices.push_back(static_cast<idx_t>(i));
    for (const auto& child : tree.children) {
        if (child) flattenOctree(*child, nodes, indices);
    }
    if (nodes.size() > UINT32_MAX || indices.size() > UINT32_MAX)
        throw std::runtime_error("Octree exceeds device index range");
    nodes[here].escape = static_cast<uint32_t>(nodes.size());
}

__device__ bool intersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, const FlatNode& node) {
    Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;
    Vec3 c = p1 + d - node.center;
    Vec3 ad = {fabsf(d.x), fabsf(d.y), fabsf(d.z)};
    const Vec3& h = node.halfExtent;
    if (fabsf(c.x) > h.x + ad.x || fabsf(c.y) > h.y + ad.y || fabsf(c.z) > h.z + ad.z) return false;
    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z - d.z*c.y) > h.y*ad.z + h.z*ad.y + EPSILON) return false;
    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x - d.x*c.z) > h.z*ad.x + h.x*ad.z + EPSILON) return false;
    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y - d.y*c.x) > h.x*ad.y + h.y*ad.x + EPSILON) return false;
    return true;
}

__device__ val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir, const Triangle& tri) {
    Vec3 e1 = tri.b - tri.a, e2 = tri.c - tri.a;
    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);
    if (fabsf(det) < EPSILON) return FLT_MAX;
    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
    Vec3 tvec = orig - tri.a;
    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
    if (u < ZERO || u > ONE) return FLT_MAX;
    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
    if (v < ZERO || u + v > ONE) return FLT_MAX;
    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
}

__device__ bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
                            const Triangle* triangles, const FlatNode* nodes,
                            const idx_t* indices, idx_t src, idx_t dst) {
    Vec3 dir = to - from;
    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;
    // Depth-first escape links preserve the original traversal with no per-ray stack.
    uint32_t node = 0;
    while (node < nodes[0].escape) {
        const FlatNode& n = nodes[node];
        if (node && !intersectsBox(from, to, n)) {
            node = n.escape;
            continue;
        }
        for (uint32_t k = 0; k < n.count; ++k) {
            idx_t i = indices[n.begin + k];
            if (i == src || i == dst) continue;
            val_t distance = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, triangles[i]);
            if (distance > EPSILON && distance < rayLen - EPSILON) return true;
        }
        ++node;
    }
    return false;
}

// Exact MT19937 checkpoints: culling consumes no samples, and every surviving
// pair consumes 16 * 4 words. Rank/thread count never changes the sample stream.
struct MTState {
    uint32_t words[624];
    int position;
    explicit MTState(uint32_t seed = 42) : position(624) {
        words[0] = seed;
        for (uint32_t i = 1; i < 624; ++i)
            words[i] = 1812433253u * (words[i-1] ''^ (words[i-1] >> 30)) + i;
    }
    void twist() {
        for (int begin = 0; begin < 623; begin += 227) {
            int end = std::min(begin + 227, 623);
            // Within each range all sources precede or follow the whole range.
            #pragma omp simd
            for (int k = begin; k < end; ++k) {
                uint32_t y = (words[k] & 0x80000000u) | (words[k+1] & 0x7fffffffu);
                words[k] = words[k < 227 ? k+397 : k-227] ''^ (y >> 1) ''^ ((y & 1u) * 0x9908b0dfu);
            }
        }
        uint32_t y = (words[623] & 0x80000000u) | (words[0] & 0x7fffffffu);
        words[623] = words[396] ''^ (y >> 1) ''^ ((y & 1u) * 0x9908b0dfu);
        position = 0;
    }
    void discard(uint64_t count) {
        while (count > static_cast<uint64_t>(624 - position)) {
            count -= 624 - position;
            twist();
        }
        position += static_cast<int>(count);
    }
};

__device__ void twistMT(uint32_t* words) {
    for (int begin = 0; begin < 623; begin += 227) {
        int k = begin + threadIdx.x;
        int end = min(begin + 227, 623);
        uint32_t result[2];
        #pragma unroll
        for (int q = 0; q < 2; ++q) {
            int i = k + q * blockDim.x;
            if (i < end) {
                uint32_t y = (words[i] & 0x80000000u) | (words[i+1] & 0x7fffffffu);
                result[q] = words[i < 227 ? i+397 : i-227] ''^ (y >> 1) ''^ ((y & 1u) * 0x9908b0dfu);
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
        #pragma unroll
        for (int q = 0; q < 2; ++q) {
            int i = k + q * blockDim.x;
            if (i < end) words[i] = result[q];
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    if (!threadIdx.x) {
        uint32_t y = (words[623] & 0x80000000u) | (words[0] & 0x7fffffffu);
        words[623] = words[396] ''^ (y >> 1) ''^ ((y & 1u) * 0x9908b0dfu);
    }
    __syncthreads();
}

__device__ val_t uniformMT(uint32_t value) {
    value ''^= value >> 11;
    value ''^= (value << 7) & 0x9d2c5680u;
    value ''^= (value << 15) & 0xefc60000u;
    value ''^= value >> 18;
    // Match std::uniform_real_distribution<float>, including its upper clamp.
    val_t result = static_cast<val_t>(value) * (1.0f / 4294967296.0f);
    return result < ONE ? result : 0x1.fffffep-1f;
}

__device__ Vec3 sampleTriangle(const Triangle& tri, val_t u, val_t v) {
    if (u + v > ONE) { u = ONE - u; v = ONE - v; }
    return tri.a + (tri.b - tri.a) * u + (tri.c - tri.a) * v;
}

__device__ val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
    val_t norm = v.norm();
    return norm <= EPSILON ? ZERO : fmaxf(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / norm);
}

struct Pair { idx_t i, j; };
constexpr size_t PAIRS_PER_BLOCK = 64;
constexpr size_t PAIRS_PER_BATCH = 262144;

__global__ void formFactorKernel(const Triangle* triangles, const FlatNode* nodes,
                                 const idx_t* indices, const val_t* areas,
                                 const Pair* pairs, const MTState* checkpoints,
                                 size_t pairCount, size_t firstRow, size_t rows,
                                 val_t* coupling, unsigned long long* nonzero) {
    __shared__ uint32_t words[624];
    __shared__ int position;
    __shared__ val_t samples[512], contributions[128];
    __shared__ unsigned counts[8];
    const MTState& checkpoint = checkpoints[blockIdx.x];
    for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < 624; k += blockDim.x) words[k] = checkpoint.words[k];
    if (!threadIdx.x) position = checkpoint.position;
    __syncthreads();
    unsigned count = 0;
    const int ray = threadIdx.x % NUM_RAYS, pairInGroup = threadIdx.x / NUM_RAYS;
    const size_t blockStart = size_t(blockIdx.x) * PAIRS_PER_BLOCK;
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < PAIRS_PER_BLOCK && blockStart + offset < pairCount; offset += 8) {
        int active = static_cast<int>(min(size_t(8), pairCount - blockStart - offset));
        int required = active * NUM_RAYS * 4;
        int copied = 0;
        while (copied < required) {
            if (position == 624) {
                twistMT(words);
                if (!threadIdx.x) position = 0;
                __syncthreads();
            }
            int chunk = min(required - copied, 624 - position);
            for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < chunk; k += blockDim.x)
                samples[copied+k] = uniformMT(words[position+k]);
            __syncthreads();
            if (!threadIdx.x) position += chunk;
            __syncthreads();
            copied += chunk;
        }
        val_t contribution = ZERO;
        Pair pair{};
        if (pairInGroup < active) {
            pair = pairs[blockStart + offset + pairInGroup];
            const Triangle& a = triangles[pair.i];
            const Triangle& b = triangles[pair.j];
            int s = pairInGroup * 64 + ray * 4;
            Vec3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, samples[s], samples[s+1]);
            Vec3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, samples[s+2], samples[s+3]);
            if (!isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, triangles, nodes, indices, pair.i, pair.j)) {
                Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
                val_t distanceSquared = v.squaredNorm();
                if (distanceSquared >= EPSILON) {
                    val_t ca = cosPhi(v, a.normal()), cb = cosPhi(-v, b.normal());
                    if (ca > ZERO && cb > ZERO) contribution = (ca * cb) / (PI * distanceSquared);
                }
            }
        }
        contributions[threadIdx.x] = contribution;
        __syncthreads();
        if (!ray && pairInGroup < active) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;
            // Preserve the sequential ray sum rather than changing its rounding.
            for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) sum += contributions[threadIdx.x+r];
            val_t kij = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
            count += kij > EPSILON;
            coupling[size_t(pair.j) * rows + pair.i - firstRow] = fminf(kij * areas[pair.j], ONE);
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    if (!ray) counts[pairInGroup] = count;
    __syncthreads();
    if (!threadIdx.x) {
        unsigned sum = 0;
        for (unsigned c : counts) sum += c;
        atomicAdd(nonzero, static_cast<unsigned long long>(sum));
    }
}

__global__ void delayKernel(const Vec3* centers, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
                            size_t rows, int* delays, int* minimum) {
    __shared__ int mins[256];
    int localMin = INT_MAX;
    for (size_t k = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         k < n * rows; k += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        size_t i = firstRow + k % rows, j = k / rows;
        int delay = i == j ? 0 : static_cast<int>(ceilf((centers[i] - centers[j]).norm() * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
        delays[k] = delay;
        if (i != j) localMin = min(localMin, delay);
    }
    mins[threadIdx.x] = localMin;
    __syncthreads();
    for (int stride = 128; stride; stride /= 2) {
        if (threadIdx.x < stride) mins[threadIdx.x] = min(mins[threadIdx.x], mins[threadIdx.x+stride]);
        __syncthreads();
    }
    if (!threadIdx.x) atomicMin(minimum, mins[0]);
}

__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* coupling, const int* delays,
                                  val_t* history, val_t* localHistory, val_t* batch,
                                  size_t n, size_t rows, size_t firstRow, size_t time,
                                  size_t steps, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
                                  val_t rho, bool singleRank) {
    size_t step = blockIdx.y;
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < rows;
         i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        size_t t = time + step;
        val_t sum = ZERO;
        // Adjacent threads handle adjacent receivers: column-major matrices
        // give coalesced loads without reordering the emitter summation.
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            size_t k = j * rows + i;
            int delay = delays[k];
            if (i + firstRow == j || t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
            val_t weight = coupling[k];
            if (weight <= ZERO) continue;
            val_t radiosity = history[(t - delay) * n + j];
            if (radiosity > ZERO) sum += weight * radiosity;
        }
        val_t emission = i + firstRow == source && t < timesteps / 2 ? ONE : ZERO;
        val_t result = rho * sum + emission;
        localHistory[i * timesteps + t] = result;
        if (singleRank) history[t * n + i] = result;
        else batch[i * steps + step] = result;
    }
}

__global__ void unpackKernel(const val_t* batch, val_t* history, size_t n,
                             size_t time, size_t steps) {
    for (size_t k = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; k < n * steps;
         k += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x)
        history[(time + k % steps) * n + k / steps] = batch[k];
}

__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* localHistory, const val_t* history,
                               size_t n, size_t rows, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
                               val_t* distances, unsigned long long* received) {
    __shared__ val_t scores[128];
    __shared__ size_t lags[128];
    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x; i < rows; i += gridDim.x) {
        val_t best = ZERO;
        size_t lag = 0;
        for (size_t t = threadIdx.x; t < timesteps; t += blockDim.x) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;
            for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
                sum += history[(tt - t) * n + source] * localHistory[i * timesteps + tt];
            if (sum > best || (sum == best && t < lag)) { best = sum; lag = t; }
        }
        scores[threadIdx.x] = best;
        lags[threadIdx.x] = lag;
        __syncthreads();
        for (int stride = 64; stride; stride /= 2) {
            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
                val_t other = scores[threadIdx.x+stride];
                size_t otherLag = lags[threadIdx.x+stride];
                if (other > scores[threadIdx.x] || (other == scores[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < lags[threadIdx.x])) {
                    scores[threadIdx.x] = other;
                    lags[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
                }
            }
            __syncthreads();
        }
        if (!threadIdx.x) {
            distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
            for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
                if (localHistory[i * timesteps + t] > EPSILON) {
                    atomicAdd(received, 1ULL);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
}

'"''' + s[end:]
start=s.index('struct SimulationState {')
end=s.index('// ============================================================================\\n// Validation',start)
s=s[:start]+r'''struct SimulationState {
    size_t numTriangles = 0, numTimesteps = 0, sourceIndex = 0;
    size_t firstRow = 0, rows = 0;
    int rank = 0, ranks = 1, minimumDelay = 1;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    unsigned long long nonZeroKij = 0, receivedEnergy = 0;
    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
    std::vector<val_t> areas, distances;
    std::vector<int> rowCounts, rowStarts;
    std::vector<uint64_t> eligibleRows;
    Octree octree;
    DeviceArray<Triangle> dTriangles;
    DeviceArray<FlatNode> dNodes;
    DeviceArray<idx_t> dIndices;
    DeviceArray<Vec3> dCenters;
    DeviceArray<val_t> dAreas, dCoupling, dHistory, dLocalHistory, dBatch, dDistances;
    DeviceArray<int> dDelays, dMinimum;
    DeviceArray<unsigned long long> dNonzero, dReceived;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    ~SimulationState() { if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
};

int gridSize(size_t n, int threads = 256) {
    return static_cast<int>(std::min(size_t(65535), (n + threads - 1) / threads));
}

void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, const ParallelRuntime& runtime,
                          int subdivisions, size_t timesteps, size_t source, val_t rho) {
    state.rank = runtime.rank;
    state.ranks = runtime.ranks;
    state.reflectivity = rho;
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);
    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
    const size_t n = state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
    state.sourceIndex = source % n;
    state.rowCounts.resize(state.ranks);
    state.rowStarts.resize(state.ranks);
    for (int r = 0; r < state.ranks; ++r) {
        state.rowStarts[r] = static_cast<int>(n * r / state.ranks);
        state.rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(n * (r+1) / state.ranks - state.rowStarts[r]);
    }
    state.firstRow = state.rowStarts[state.rank];
    state.rows = state.rowCounts[state.rank];
    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\nBuilding octree...\\n\", n);
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp single
        state.octree.build(state.triangles);
    }
    std::vector<FlatNode> nodes;
    std::vector<idx_t> indices;
    flattenOctree(state.octree, nodes, indices);
    std::vector<Vec3> centers(n);
    state.areas.resize(n);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
        centers[i] = state.triangles[i].center();
    }
    state.eligibleRows.resize(n);
    std::vector<uint64_t> localEligible(state.rows);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t row = 0; row < state.rows; ++row) {
        size_t i = state.firstRow + row;
        uint64_t count = 0;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j)
            count += i "'!= j && !(state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f);
        localEligible[row] = count;
    }
    mpiCheck(MPI_Allgatherv(localEligible.data(), static_cast<int>(state.rows), MPI_UINT64_T,
                           state.eligibleRows.data(), state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowStarts.data(),
                           MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    cudaCheck(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&state.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    state.dTriangles.resize(n); state.dCenters.resize(n); state.dAreas.resize(n);
    state.dNodes.resize(nodes.size()); state.dIndices.resize(indices.size());
    state.dCoupling.resize(state.rows * n); state.dDelays.resize(state.rows * n);
    state.dHistory.resize(timesteps * n); state.dLocalHistory.resize(timesteps * state.rows);
    state.dDistances.resize(state.rows); state.dMinimum.resize(1);
    state.dNonzero.resize(1); state.dReceived.resize(1);
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(state.dTriangles.get(), state.triangles.data(), n*sizeof(Triangle), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(state.dCenters.get(), centers.data(), n*sizeof(Vec3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(state.dAreas.get(), state.areas.data(), n*sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(state.dNodes.get(), nodes.data(), nodes.size()*sizeof(FlatNode), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(state.dIndices.get(), indices.data(), indices.size()*sizeof(idx_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
    if (state.rows) cudaCheck(cudaMemsetAsync(state.dCoupling.get(), 0, state.rows*n*sizeof(val_t), state.stream));
    if (timesteps) cudaCheck(cudaMemsetAsync(state.dHistory.get(), 0, timesteps*n*sizeof(val_t), state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaMemsetAsync(state.dNonzero.get(), 0, sizeof(unsigned long long), state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaMemsetAsync(state.dReceived.get(), 0, sizeof(unsigned long long), state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(state.stream));
    if (state.rank == 0) state.distances.resize(n);
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    if (!state.rank) printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...'"\\n\");
    int minimum = INT_MAX;
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(state.dMinimum.get(), &minimum, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
    if (state.rows) {
        delayKernel<<<gridSize(state.rows * state.numTriangles), 256, 0, state.stream>>>(
            state.dCenters.get(), state.numTriangles, state.firstRow, state.rows,
            state.dDelays.get(), state.dMinimum.get());
        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
    }
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(&minimum, state.dMinimum.get(), sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(state.stream));
    mpiCheck(MPI_Allreduce(&minimum, &state.minimumDelay, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    if (state.minimumDelay <= 0) throw std::runtime_error(\"Distinct mesh triangles must have a positive propagation delay\");
}

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    if ("'!state.rank) printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on distributed CUDA accelerators...'"\\n\");
    uint64_t prefix = 0, remaining = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.firstRow; ++i) prefix += state.eligibleRows[i];
    for (size_t i = state.firstRow; i < state.firstRow + state.rows; ++i) remaining += state.eligibleRows[i];
    MTState rng;
    rng.discard(prefix * NUM_RAYS * 4);
    const size_t capacity = static_cast<size_t>(std::min(uint64_t(PAIRS_PER_BATCH), remaining));
    if (capacity) {
        PinnedArray<Pair> hostPairs[2];
        PinnedArray<MTState> hostCheckpoints[2];
        DeviceArray<Pair> devicePairs[2];
        DeviceArray<MTState> deviceCheckpoints[2];
        cudaEvent_t ready[2];
        const size_t checkpointCapacity = (capacity + PAIRS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / PAIRS_PER_BLOCK;
        for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {
            hostPairs[b].resize(capacity); hostCheckpoints[b].resize(checkpointCapacity);
            devicePairs[b].resize(capacity); deviceCheckpoints[b].resize(checkpointCapacity);
            cudaCheck(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ready[b], cudaEventDisableTiming));
            cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(ready[b], state.stream));
        }
        size_t i = state.firstRow, j = 0, batchNumber = 0;
        while (remaining) {
            int b = batchNumber++ % 2;
            cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(ready[b]));
            size_t count = static_cast<size_t>(std::min(uint64_t(capacity), remaining));
            for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
                while (i == j || state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f) {
                    if (++j == state.numTriangles) { ++i; j = 0; }
                }
                hostPairs[b].get()[k] = {static_cast<idx_t>(i), static_cast<idx_t>(j)};
                if (++j == state.numTriangles) { ++i; j = 0; }
            }
            const size_t blocks = (count + PAIRS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / PAIRS_PER_BLOCK;
            for (size_t block = 0; block < blocks; ++block) {
                hostCheckpoints[b].get()[block] = rng;
                rng.discard(std::min(PAIRS_PER_BLOCK, count - block*PAIRS_PER_BLOCK) * NUM_RAYS * 4);
            }
            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(devicePairs[b].get(), hostPairs[b].get(), count*sizeof(Pair), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceCheckpoints[b].get(), hostCheckpoints[b].get(), blocks*sizeof(MTState), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
            formFactorKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(blocks), 128, 0, state.stream>>>(
                state.dTriangles.get(), state.dNodes.get(), state.dIndices.get(), state.dAreas.get(),
                devicePairs[b].get(), deviceCheckpoints[b].get(), count, state.firstRow, state.rows,
                state.dCoupling.get(), state.dNonzero.get());
            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
            cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(ready[b], state.stream));
            remaining -= count;
        }
        cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(state.stream));
        for (auto event : ready) cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(event));
    }
    unsigned long long localNonzero = 0;
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&localNonzero, state.dNonzero.get(), sizeof(localNonzero), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    mpiCheck(MPI_Allreduce(&localNonzero, &state.nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
}

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    if ("'!state.rank) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...'"\\n\");
    const size_t n = state.numTriangles, timesteps = state.numTimesteps;
    if ("'!timesteps) return;
    // Every read precedes this entire batch, so steps can run concurrently.
    const size_t batchLimit = std::min({size_t(state.minimumDelay), timesteps, size_t(INT_MAX) / n, size_t(65535)});
    PinnedArray<val_t> send, receive;
    if (state.ranks > 1) {
        send.resize(state.rows * batchLimit); receive.resize(n * batchLimit);
        state.dBatch.resize(n * batchLimit);
    }
    std::vector<int> counts(state.ranks), starts(state.ranks);
    for (size_t time = 0; time < timesteps; time += batchLimit) {
        size_t steps = std::min(batchLimit, timesteps - time);
        if (state.rows) {
            propagationKernel<<<dim3(gridSize(state.rows, 128), static_cast<unsigned>(steps)), 128, 0, state.stream>>>(
                state.dCoupling.get(), state.dDelays.get(), state.dHistory.get(), state.dLocalHistory.get(),
                state.dBatch.get(), n, state.rows, state.firstRow, time, steps, timesteps,
                state.sourceIndex, state.reflectivity, state.ranks == 1);
            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
        }
        if (state.ranks > 1) {
            if (state.rows) cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(send.get(), state.dBatch.get(), state.rows*steps*sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, state.stream));
            cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(state.stream));
            for (int r = 0; r < state.ranks; ++r) {
                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(state.rowCounts[r] * steps);
                starts[r] = static_cast<int>(state.rowStarts[r] * steps);
            }
            mpiCheck(MPI_Allgatherv(send.get(), counts[state.rank], MPI_FLOAT, receive.get(),
                                   counts.data(), starts.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(state.dBatch.get(), receive.get(), n*steps*sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, state.stream));
            unpackKernel<<<gridSize(n * steps), 256, 0, state.stream>>>(state.dBatch.get(), state.dHistory.get(), n, time, steps);
            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
            // The receive buffer is reused by MPI at the next batch.
            cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(state.stream));
        }
        if (!state.rank && ((time + steps) % 10 == 0 || time + steps == timesteps))
            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu'"\\n\", time + steps, timesteps);
    }
    cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(state.stream));
}

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    if ("'!state.rank) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...'"\\n\");
    if (state.rows) {
        distanceKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(state.rows, size_t(65535))), 128, 0, state.stream>>>(
            state.dLocalHistory.get(), state.dHistory.get(), state.numTriangles, state.rows,
            state.numTimesteps, state.sourceIndex, state.dDistances.get(), state.dReceived.get());
        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
    }
    std::vector<val_t> localDistances(state.rows);
    if (state.rows) cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(localDistances.data(), state.dDistances.get(), state.rows*sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, state.stream));
    unsigned long long received = 0;
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(&received, state.dReceived.get(), sizeof(received), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, state.stream));
    cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(state.stream));
    mpiCheck(MPI_Gatherv(localDistances.data(), static_cast<int>(state.rows), MPI_FLOAT,
                        state.distances.data(), state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowStarts.data(),
                        MPI_FLOAT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    mpiCheck(MPI_Allreduce(&received, &state.receivedEnergy, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
}

// ============================================================================

using idx_t = uint32_t;
using val_t = float;

constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling
constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;

// ============================================================================
// Vector and Triangle Types
// ============================================================================

struct Vec3 {
    val_t x, y, z;

    __host__ __device__ constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    __host__ __device__ constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    __host__ __device__ explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}

    __host__ __device__ Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    __host__ __device__ Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    __host__ __device__ Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
    __host__ __device__ Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }
    __host__ __device__ Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }

    __host__ __device__ val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
    __host__ __device__ Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {
        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};
    }

    __host__ __device__ val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }
    __host__ __device__ val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }
    __host__ __device__ Vec3 normalized() const {
        val_t n = norm();
        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();
    }

    __host__ __device__ bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {
        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;
    }
};

struct Triangle {
    Vec3 a, b, c;
    Vec3 _normal;

    __host__ __device__ Triangle() = default;
    __host__ __device__ Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)
        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}

    __host__ __device__ Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }
    __host__ __device__ Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }

    __host__ __device__ val_t area() const {
        Vec3 ab = b - a;
        Vec3 ac = c - a;
        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();
    }

    __host__ __device__ bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)
// ============================================================================

// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap
bool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {
    // Translate triangle to box center
    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;
    printf("  Non-zero form factors: %llu/%zu (%.2f%%)\n", state.nonZeroKij,
           state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
           100.0f * state.nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));
    if (state.nonZeroKij == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    if (!allNonNegative) {
        return false;
    }

    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Hash for Verification
// ============================================================================

uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        uint32_t bits;
        std::memcpy(&bits, &state.distances[i], sizeof(bits));
        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(bits) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
// ============================================================================

int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
    int subdivisions = 0;
    int triangles = 20;
    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
        subdivisions++;
        triangles *= 4;
    }
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ParallelRuntime runtime(argc, argv);
    try {
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (!runtime.rank) printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (!runtime.rank) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            return 1;
        }
    }

    if (timesteps < 0) throw std::runtime_error("Timesteps must be non-negative");
    runtime.selectDevice();
    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
    if (!runtime.rank) {

    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");

    printf("MPI ranks: %d; OpenMP threads per rank: %d; CUDA enabled\n", runtime.ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
    }
    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, runtime, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    if (!runtime.rank) printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    double startPre = MPI_Wtime();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);

    mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    long preDuration = static_cast<long>((MPI_Wtime() - startPre) * 1000);
    if (!runtime.rank) printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n\n", preDuration);

    // Simulation
    double startSim = MPI_Wtime();

    runSimulation(state);

    mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    long simDuration = static_cast<long>((MPI_Wtime() - startSim) * 1000);
    if (!runtime.rank) printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n\n", simDuration);

    // Distance computation
    double startDist = MPI_Wtime();

    computeDistances(state);

    mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    long distDuration = static_cast<long>((MPI_Wtime() - startDist) * 1000);
    if (!runtime.rank) {
    printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n\n", distDuration);

    // Total time
    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

    // Hash
    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);

s=Path('roomsim/roomsim.cpp').read_text()
s=s[:s.index('// ============================================================================\\n// Hybrid runtime')]
s=s.replace('#include <cuda_runtime.h>','').replace('__host__ __device__ ','')
s += r'''
#include <random>
Vec3 sample(const Triangle& tri, std::mt19937& rng) {
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> dist(0,1);
    float u=dist(rng),v=dist(rng);
    if(u+v>1) {u=1-u;v=1-v;}
    return tri.a+(tri.b-tri.a)*u+(tri.c-tri.a)*v;
}
float intersect(const Vec3& orig,const Vec3& dir,const Triangle&tri) {
    Vec3 e1=tri.b-tri.a,e2=tri.c-tri.a,pvec=dir.cross(e2);
    float det=e1.dot(pvec);
    if(std::abs(det)<EPSILON) return FLT_MAX;
    float invDet=1.0f/det;
    Vec3 tvec=orig-tri.a;
    float u=tvec.dot(pvec)*invDet;
    if(u<0||u>1) return FLT_MAX;
    Vec3 qvec=tvec.cross(e1);
    float v=dir.dot(qvec)*invDet;
    if(v<0||u+v>1) return FLT_MAX;
    return e2.dot(qvec)*invDet;
}
float cosPhi(const Vec3&v,const Vec3&normal) {
    float vNorm=v.norm();
    return vNorm<=EPSILON?ZERO:std::max(ZERO,v.dot(normal)/vNorm);
}
int main(int argc,char**argv) {
    int subdivisions=atoi(argv[1]),T=atoi(argv[2]),source=atoi(argv[3]);
    float rho=atof(argv[4]);
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions,10);
    auto&triangles=mesh.triangles;
    size_t N=triangles.size();
    source=static_cast<size_t>(source)%N;
    Octree tree;
    tree.build(triangles);
    std::vector<float> areas(N),K(N*N),B(T*N),D(N);
    std::vector<int> tau(N*N);
    std::mt19937 rng(42);
    for(size_t i=0;i<N;++i) areas[i]=triangles[i].area();
    for(size_t i=0;i<N;++i) for(size_t j=0;j<N;++j) {
        if(i==j) continue;
        tau[i*N+j]=std::ceil((triangles[i].center()-triangles[j].center()).norm()*INV_WAVE_SPEED);
        if(triangles[i].normal().dot(triangles[j].normal())>0.99f) continue;
        float kij=ZERO;
        for(int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
            Vec3 a=sample(triangles[i],rng),b=sample(triangles[j],rng),dir=b-a;
            float len=dir.norm();
            if(len<EPSILON) continue;
            Vec3 dn=dir/len;
            bool blocked=tree.applyToTris(a,b,[&](size_t idx,const Triangle&t){
                if(idx==i||idx==j) return false;
                float d=intersect(a,dn,t);
                return d>EPSILON&&d<len-EPSILON;
            });
            if(blocked) continue;
            float ds=dir.squaredNorm();
            if(ds<EPSILON) continue;
            float ca=cosPhi(dir,triangles[i].normal()),cb=cosPhi(-dir,triangles[j].normal());
            if(ca<=ZERO||cb<=ZERO) continue;
            kij+=(ca*cb)/(PI*ds);
        }
        K[i*N+j]=kij*INV_NUM_RAYS;
    }
    for(int t=0;t<T;++t) for(size_t i=0;i<N;++i) {
        float sum=ZERO;
        for(size_t j=0;j<N;++j) {
            if(i==j||t<tau[i*N+j]||K[i*N+j]<=ZERO) continue;
            float rad=B[(t-tau[i*N+j])*N+j];
            if(rad<=ZERO) continue;
            sum+=std::min(K[i*N+j]*areas[j],ONE)*rad;
        }
        B[t*N+i]=rho*sum+(int(i)==source&&t<T/2?ONE:ZERO);
    }
    for(size_t i=0;i<N;++i) {
        float maxCorr=ZERO;
        int bestT=0;
        for(int t=0;t<T;++t) {
            float sum=ZERO;
            for(int tt=t;tt<T;++tt) sum+=B[(tt-t)*N+source]*B[tt*N+i];
            if(sum>maxCorr) {maxCorr=sum;bestT=t;}
        }
        D[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
    }
    print_results(std::vector<double>(D.begin(),D.end()),\"Distances\");
    for(size_t i=0;i<N;++i) printf(\"D %zu %g\\n\",i,D[i]);
    print_results(K,\"Kij\");
    print_results(B,\"Radiosity\");
    return 0;
}
'''

        return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z;

    }''')

        return {fmaf(y, o.z, -z * o.y), fmaf(z, o.x, -x * o.z), fmaf(x, o.y, -y * o.x)};

codex
The difference comes from multiply-add rounding: the original CPU build uses fused operations. I’ve made those operations explicit in the CUDA math and am checking hashes across MPI rank counts, uneven partitions, and short runs.

codex
The checked hashes now match, including the larger case. Scaling tests exposed another bottleneck: advancing the original random stream serially. I’m adding exact MT19937 jump-ahead and OpenMP preparation of independent work chunks so additional GPUs can stay busy without changing the samples.

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('roomsim/roomsim.cpp');s=p.read_text().replace('#include <array>','#include <array>\\n#include <exception>')
needle='''    void discard(uint64_t count) {
        while (count > static_cast<uint64_t>(624 - position)) {
            count -= 624 - position;
            twist();
        }
        position += static_cast<int>(count);
    }
};'''
replacement=needle[:-3]+r'''
    void jump(uint64_t count) {
        // Characteristic polynomial of the MT19937 word recurrence (degree
        // 19937, 135 terms). Apply x"'^count modulo this polynomial to the
        // canonical window of 624 consecutive untempered stream words.
        static constexpr int degree = 19937;
        static constexpr int powers[] = {
            19937,19314,19087,18860,18691,18633,18406,18237,18179,18068,17952,
            17841,17783,17725,17498,17445,17329,17271,17160,17044,16933,16875,
            16822,16817,16595,16590,16537,16421,16368,16363,16252,16141,16136,
            16025,15967,15909,15682,15629,15576,15513,15455,15349,15344,15228,
            15117,15059,15006,15001,14953,14779,14774,14721,14605,14552,14547,
            14436,14325,14320,14209,14151,14093,13866,13813,13760,13697,13639,
            13533,13528,13412,13301,13243,13190,13185,13137,12963,12958,12905,
            12789,12736,12731,12673,12620,12509,12504,12393,12335,12277,11997,
            11944,11881,11838,11717,11712,11611,11485,11384,11374,11321,11215,
            11157,11147,11089,10920,10761,10693,10128,9969,9901,9505,8206,
            7979,7752,7583,7525,7477,7129,6569,6337,5661,4753,4362,4135,3908,
            3681,3454,3227,3000,2773,2493,1870,1643,1585,1416,1189,0
        };
        std::array<uint64_t, 312> polynomial{};
        polynomial[0] = 1;
        int highest = 63;
        while (highest >= 0 && !(count & (uint64_t(1) << highest))) --highest;
        for (int bit = highest; bit >= 0; --bit) {
            std::array<uint64_t, 624> squared{};
            for (int i = 0; i < degree; ++i)
                if ((polynomial[i/64] >> (i%64)) & 1u)
                    squared[(2*i)/64] |= uint64_t(1) << ((2*i)%64);
            for (int i = 2*degree-2; i >= degree; --i) {
                if ((squared[i/64] >> (i%64)) & 1u) {
                    for (int power : powers) {
                        int k = i-degree+power;
                        squared[k/64] ''^= uint64_t(1) << (k%64);
                    }
                }
            }
            std::copy_n(squared.begin(), polynomial.size(), polynomial.begin());
            if ((count >> bit) & 1u) {
                uint64_t carry = 0;
                for (auto& word : polynomial) {
                    uint64_t next = word >> 63;
                    word = (word << 1) | carry;
                    carry = next;
                }
                if ((polynomial[degree/64] >> (degree%64)) & 1u)
                    for (int power : powers)
                        polynomial[power/64] ''^= uint64_t(1) << (power%64);
            }
        }
        // A complete twist projects the seeded state into the invariant
        // 19937-bit state space and supplies the first raw output window.
        twist();
        uint32_t result[624]{};
        int head = 0;
        for (int bit = 0; bit < degree; ++bit) {
            if ((polynomial[bit/64] >> (bit%64)) & 1u) {
                int first = 624-head;
                #pragma omp simd
                for (int k = 0; k < first; ++k) result[k] ''^= words[head+k];
                #pragma omp simd
                for (int k = first; k < 624; ++k) result[k] ''^= words[k-first];
            }
            uint32_t y = (words[head] & 0x80000000u) | (words[(head+1)%624] & 0x7fffffffu);
            words[head] = words[(head+397)%624] ''^ (y >> 1) ''^ ((y & 1u) * 0x9908b0dfu);
            head = (head+1)%624;
        }
        std::copy_n(result, 624, words);
        position = 0;
    }
    void seek(uint64_t count) {
        // Only called on a freshly seeded generator. Short skips are cheaper
        // than polynomial evaluation; large skips have logarithmic cost.
        if (count < 64000000) discard(count);
        else jump(count);
    }
};'"'''
assert needle in s
s=s.replace(needle,replacement)
start=s.index('void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {')
end=s.index('void runSimulation(SimulationState& state)',start)
s=s[:start]+r'''void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    if ("'!state.rank) printf("Computing form factors (Kij) on distributed CUDA accelerators...'"\\n\");
    uint64_t prefix = 0, total = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.firstRow; ++i) prefix += state.eligibleRows[i];
    for (size_t i = state.firstRow; i < state.firstRow + state.rows; ++i) total += state.eligibleRows[i];
    int device = 0;
    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device));
    const int workers = static_cast<int>(std::min({uint64_t(omp_get_max_threads()), uint64_t(8),
                                                  std::max(uint64_t(1), (total+65535)/65536)}));
    std::exception_ptr failure;
    // Each worker owns a contiguous part of the original stream, two bounded
    // staging buffers, and a CUDA stream. GPU tracing overlaps CPU preparation
    // and transfers, without a serial random-number bottleneck on rank zero.
    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workers)
    {
        try {
            cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(device));
            const uint64_t begin = total * omp_get_thread_num() / omp_get_num_threads();
            const uint64_t end = total * (omp_get_thread_num()+1) / omp_get_num_threads();
            uint64_t remaining = end-begin;
            if (remaining) {
                MTState rng;
                rng.seek((prefix+begin) * NUM_RAYS * 4);
                size_t i = state.firstRow, j = 0;
                uint64_t skipped = 0;
                while (skipped + state.eligibleRows[i] <= begin) skipped += state.eligibleRows[i++];
                while (skipped < begin) {
                    if (i "'!= j && !(state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f)) ++skipped;
                    ++j;
                }
                const size_t capacity = static_cast<size_t>(std::min(
                    uint64_t(PAIRS_PER_BATCH / workers / PAIRS_PER_BLOCK * PAIRS_PER_BLOCK), remaining));
                PinnedArray<Pair> hostPairs[2];
                PinnedArray<MTState> hostCheckpoints[2];
                DeviceArray<Pair> devicePairs[2];
                DeviceArray<MTState> deviceCheckpoints[2];
                struct WorkerStream {
                    cudaStream_t value = nullptr;
                    ~WorkerStream() { if (value) cudaStreamDestroy(value); }
                } stream;
                cudaCheck(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream.value, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
                cudaEvent_t ready[2];
                const size_t checkpointCapacity = (capacity+PAIRS_PER_BLOCK-1)/PAIRS_PER_BLOCK;
                for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {
                    hostPairs[b].resize(capacity); hostCheckpoints[b].resize(checkpointCapacity);
                    devicePairs[b].resize(capacity); deviceCheckpoints[b].resize(checkpointCapacity);
                    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ready[b], cudaEventDisableTiming));
                    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(ready[b], stream.value));
                }
                size_t batchNumber = 0;
                while (remaining) {
                    int b = batchNumber++ % 2;
                    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(ready[b]));
                    size_t count = static_cast<size_t>(std::min(uint64_t(capacity), remaining));
                    for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
                        while (i == j || state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f) {
                            if (++j == state.numTriangles) { ++i; j = 0; }
                        }
                        hostPairs[b].get()[k] = {static_cast<idx_t>(i), static_cast<idx_t>(j)};
                        if (++j == state.numTriangles) { ++i; j = 0; }
                    }
                    const size_t blocks = (count+PAIRS_PER_BLOCK-1)/PAIRS_PER_BLOCK;
                    for (size_t block = 0; block < blocks; ++block) {
                        hostCheckpoints[b].get()[block] = rng;
                        rng.discard(std::min(PAIRS_PER_BLOCK, count-block*PAIRS_PER_BLOCK)*NUM_RAYS*4);
                    }
                    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(devicePairs[b].get(), hostPairs[b].get(), count*sizeof(Pair), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream.value));
                    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceCheckpoints[b].get(), hostCheckpoints[b].get(), blocks*sizeof(MTState), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream.value));
                    formFactorKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(blocks), 128, 0, stream.value>>>(
                        state.dTriangles.get(), state.dNodes.get(), state.dIndices.get(), state.dAreas.get(),
                        devicePairs[b].get(), deviceCheckpoints[b].get(), count, state.firstRow, state.rows,
                        state.dCoupling.get(), state.dNonzero.get());
                    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
                    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(ready[b], stream.value));
                    remaining -= count;
                }
                cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.value));
                for (auto event : ready) cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(event));
            }
        } catch (...) {
            #pragma omp critical
            { if (!failure) failure = std::current_exception(); }
        }
    }
    if (failure) std::rethrow_exception(failure);
    unsigned long long localNonzero = 0;
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&localNonzero, state.dNonzero.get(), sizeof(localNonzero), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    mpiCheck(MPI_Allreduce(&localNonzero, &state.nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
}

struct Engine {
 using result_type=uint32_t;MTState state;

};
Vec3 sample(const Triangle&tri,Engine&rng){std::uniform_real_distribution<float>d(0,1);float u=d(rng),v=d(rng);if(u+v>1){u=1-u;v=1-v;}Vec3 ab=tri.b-tri.a,ac=tri.c-tri.a;return tri.a+ab*u+ac*v;}
float intersect(const Vec3&orig,const Vec3&dir,const Triangle&tri){
 Vec3 e1=tri.b-tri.a,e2=tri.c-tri.a,pvec=dir.cross(e2);float det=e1.dot(pvec);if(std::abs(det)<EPSILON)return FLT_MAX;

 Vec3 qvec=tvec.cross(e1);float v=dir.dot(qvec)*invDet;if(v<0||u+v>1)return FLT_MAX;return e2.dot(qvec)*invDet;
}
float cosPhi(const Vec3&v,const Vec3&normal){float n=v.norm();return n<=EPSILON?ZERO:std::max(ZERO,v.dot(normal)/n);}
float factor(size_t i,size_t j,const std::vector<Triangle>&tris,const Octree&tree,Engine&rng){
 if(tris[i].normal().dot(tris[j].normal())>0.99f)return ZERO;

  if(tree.applyToTris(a,b,[&](size_t k,const Triangle&t){if(k==i||k==j)return false;float d=intersect(a,dn,t);return d>EPSILON&&d<len-EPSILON;}))continue;
  float ds=dir.squaredNorm();if(ds<EPSILON)continue;float ca=cosPhi(dir,tris[i].normal()),cb=cosPhi(-dir,tris[j].normal());
  if(ca<=ZERO||cb<=ZERO)continue;sum+=(ca*cb)/(PI*ds);

}

  #pragma omp single
  tree.build(tris);

            for (const auto &face : faces) {
                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);
                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);
                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);

                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});
                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});
                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});
                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});
            }
            faces = std::move(newFaces);
        }

        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a "room")
        triangles.resize(faces.size());
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < faces.size(); ++i) {
            const auto &face = faces[i];
            triangles[i] = Triangle(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);
        }
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Hybrid runtime and device geometry
// ============================================================================

void mpiCheck(int error) {
    if (error == MPI_SUCCESS)
        return;
    char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
    int length = 0;
    MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
    fprintf(stderr, "MPI: %.*s\n", length, message);
    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, error);
}

void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error) {
    if (error != cudaSuccess)
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(error));
}

struct ParallelRuntime {
    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
    MPI_Comm localComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    ParallelRuntime(int &argc, char **&argv) {
        int provided = 0;
        mpiCheck(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
            fprintf(stderr, "MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED is required.\n");
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        }
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &localComm));
        if (!std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS")) {
            int localRanks = 1;
            mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_size(localComm, &localRanks));
            // Avoid every MPI rank creating a full-node OpenMP team by default.
            omp_set_num_threads(std::max(1, std::min(8, omp_get_num_procs() / localRanks)));
        }
    }
    void selectDevice() const {
        int localRank = 0, devices = 0;
        mpiCheck(MPI_Comm_rank(localComm, &localRank));
        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
        if (!devices)
            throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA accelerator is required on every rank");
        // Also supports schedulers that expose just one assigned GPU per process.
        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
    }
    ~ParallelRuntime() {
        MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
        MPI_Finalize();
    }
};

template <class T> class DeviceArray {
    T *data_ = nullptr;

  public:
    DeviceArray() = default;
    DeviceArray(const DeviceArray &) = delete;
    DeviceArray &operator=(const DeviceArray &) = delete;
    void resize(size_t n) {
        if (data_)
            cudaCheck(cudaFree(data_));
        data_ = nullptr;
        if (n > SIZE_MAX / sizeof(T))
            throw std::runtime_error("Device allocation size overflow");
        if (n)
            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void **>(&data_), n * sizeof(T)));
    }
    T *get() const { return data_; }
    ~DeviceArray() {
        if (data_)
            cudaFree(data_);
    }
};

template <class T> class PinnedArray {
    T *data_ = nullptr;

  public:
    PinnedArray() = default;
    PinnedArray(const PinnedArray &) = delete;
    PinnedArray &operator=(const PinnedArray &) = delete;
    void resize(size_t n) {
        if (data_)
            cudaCheck(cudaFreeHost(data_));
        data_ = nullptr;
        if (n > SIZE_MAX / sizeof(T))
            throw std::runtime_error("Host allocation size overflow");
        // Allocate one element even on ranks owning no receiver rows.
        cudaCheck(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void **>(&data_), std::max(n, size_t(1)) * sizeof(T)));
    }
    T *get() const { return data_; }
    ~PinnedArray() {
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    ParallelRuntime runtime(argc, argv);
    try {
        int targetTriangles = 320;
        int timesteps = 50;
        int sourceIdx = 0;
        val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
        bool validate = false;
        bool printResults = false;

        // Parse command line arguments
        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
                validate = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
                printResults = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
                if (!runtime.rank)
                    printUsage(argv[0]);
                return 0;
            } else {
                if (!runtime.rank) {
                    printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                    printUsage(argv[0]);
                }
                return 1;
            }
        }

        if (timesteps < 0)
            throw std::runtime_error("Timesteps must be non-negative");
        runtime.selectDevice();
        int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
        if (!runtime.rank) {

            printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
            printf("===================================\n");
            printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
            printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
            printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
            printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
            printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
            printf("\n");

            printf("MPI ranks: %d; OpenMP threads per rank: %d; CUDA enabled\n", runtime.ranks,
                   omp_get_max_threads());
        }
        // Initialize
        SimulationState state;
        initializeSimulation(state, runtime, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                             static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

        if (!runtime.rank)
            printf("\n");

        // Precomputation
        mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        double startPre = MPI_Wtime();

        computeTimeDelays(state);
        computeFormFactors(state);

        mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        long preDuration = static_cast<long>((MPI_Wtime() - startPre) * 1000);
        if (!runtime.rank)
            printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n\n", preDuration);

        // Simulation
        double startSim = MPI_Wtime();

        runSimulation(state);

        mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        long simDuration = static_cast<long>((MPI_Wtime() - startSim) * 1000);
        if (!runtime.rank)
            printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n\n", simDuration);

        // Distance computation
        double startDist = MPI_Wtime();

        computeDistances(state);

        mpiCheck(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        long distDuration = static_cast<long>((MPI_Wtime() - startDist) * 1000);
        uint64_t matrixMemory = state.rows * state.numTriangles * (sizeof(val_t) + sizeof(int)) +
                                state.numTimesteps * (state.numTriangles + state.rows) * sizeof(val_t);
        uint64_t maximumMatrixMemory = 0;
        mpiCheck(
            MPI_Allreduce(&matrixMemory, &maximumMatrixMemory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        if (!runtime.rank) {
            printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n\n", distDuration);

            // Total time
            long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
            printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

            // Performance metrics
            size_t n = state.numTriangles;
            size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
            double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
            double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
            double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

            printf("\nPerformance:\n");
            printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
            printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
            printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
            printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
            printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
            printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

            // Memory usage
            printf("  Matrix/history memory per rank (maximum): %.2f MB\n",
                   maximumMatrixMemory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

            // Hash
            uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
            printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
            printf("\n");

            // Print results for external validation
            if (printResults) {
                // Convert distances to double for output
                std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
                print_results(distData, "Distances");
            }
        }
        int status = 0;
        if (!runtime.rank && validate && !validateResults(state))
            status = 1;
        mpiCheck(MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        return status;
    } catch (const std::exception &error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", runtime.rank, error.what());
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
}
